{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"routs","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A group of people; a crowd, a throng, a troop; in particular (archaic), a group of people accompanying or travelling with someone."],"synonyms":[{"word":"company"},{"word":"gathering"}],"tags":["countable","obsolete"]},{"glosses":["A group of animals, especially one which is lively or unruly, or made up of wild animals such as wolves; a flock, a herd, a pack."],"tags":["archaic","collective","countable"]},{"glosses":["A group or assortment of snails."],"synonyms":[{"word":"escargatoire"}],"tags":["collective","countable"]},{"glosses":["A group of disorganized things."],"tags":["countable"]},{"glosses":["A group of (often violent) criminals or gangsters; such people as a class; (more generally) a disorderly and tumultuous crowd, a mob; hence (archaic, preceded by the), the common people as a group, the rabble."],"tags":["countable"]},{"glosses":["A fashionable assembly; a large evening party, a soirée."],"tags":["countable","dated"]},{"glosses":["A noisy disturbance; also, a disorderly argument or fight, a brawl; (uncountable) disturbance of the peace, commotion, tumult."],"tags":["archaic","countable"]},{"glosses":["An illegal assembly of people; specifically, three or more people who have come together intending to do something illegal, and who have taken steps towards this, regarded as more serious than an unlawful assembly but not as serious as a riot; the act of assembling in this manner."],"tags":["countable","historical"],"topics":["law"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɹaʊt/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[ɹʌʊt]","tags":["Canada"]}],"upstream_line":239177,"upstream_offset":1182914200,"upstream_record_sha256":"5e7783778bdf3b224b2a22176f86a3a5fadf7f9b685bc183ce2350bf528a0364","word":"rout"}

{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"routs","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The rout of the enemy was complete.","type":"example"},{"text":"The visiting football team was put to rout.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The act of completely defeating an army or other enemy force, causing it to retreat in a disorganized manner; (by extension) in politics, sport, etc.: a convincing defeat; a thrashing, a trouncing."]},{"glosses":["The retreat of an enemy force, etc., in this manner; also (archaic, rare), the army, enemy force, etc., so retreating."],"tags":["also","figuratively"],"topics":["government","military","politics","war"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɹaʊt/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[ɹʌʊt]","tags":["Canada"]}],"upstream_line":239179,"upstream_offset":1182950179,"upstream_record_sha256":"639ee2f558c521767ae2c7da2b577f4661468a41b8d9fb82ef2a6891178f58c6","word":"rout"}
